Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on complex_analysis structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 759. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 17 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. This completes the proof. QED.
